                          <td class="font_18_b" align="center">天津大学2026年硕士学位研究生招生简章</td>
                          </tr>
                        <tr>
                          <td class="font_black_14" align="center"></td>

                          <td class="nav_a5"><table width="100%" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0">
                            <tr>
                              <td class="font_black_14" id="zoom" height="450" valign="top"><div class=TRS_Editor><span style="font-size: 12pt;"><font style="">&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;</font></span>
<div><span style="font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312" style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">&nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">天津大学</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">（</font><font face="Times New Roman">Tianjin</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;"><font face="Times New Roman">University</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">简称天大，</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">其前身为北洋大学，始建于</font>1895<font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font><font face="Times New Roman">10</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">月</font><font face="Times New Roman">2</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">日，是中国第一所现代大学，素以</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">“兴学强国”的使命、“</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">实事求是</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">”</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">的校训、</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">“</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">严谨治学</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">”</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">的校风</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">爱国奉献</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">”</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">的传统</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">和</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“矢志创新”的追求，</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">享誉海内外。</font>1951<font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">由国家定名为天津大学</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，</font>1959<font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">被</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">中共中央</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">指定为国家首批重点大学</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，是</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">“</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">211<font face="仿宋_GB2312">工程</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">”、“</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">985<font face="仿宋_GB2312">工程</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">”</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">首批重点建设</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;">的</span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">大学</font></span><span style="font-size: 12pt; font-family: 仿宋_GB2312;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，入选国家</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“世界一流大学建设”</font><font face="Times New Roman">A</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">类高校，是全国首批</font><font face="Times New Roman">14</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">个“</font><font face="Times New Roman">2011</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">协同创新中心”之一。</font></span>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">天津大学目前已成为一所师资力量雄厚、学科特色鲜明、教育质量和科研水平居于国内一流、在国际上有较大影响的高水平研究型大学。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">作为全国首批学位授权自主审核单位之一，天津大学坚持</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“强工、厚理、振文、兴医、交叉（融合）”的发展理念，优化学科生态，服务国之所需，形成了工科优势明显、理工结合，经、管、文、法、医、教育、艺术等多学科协调发展的综合学科布局。现有</font><font face="Times New Roman">53</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">个一级学科硕士点，</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">43</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">个一级学科博士点。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;color:rgb(51,51,51);&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">获批国家卓越工程师学院，入选国家首批未来技术学院建设单位、首批国家储能技术产教融合创新平台、首批国家级创新创业学院、全国博士研究生教育综合改革试点高校、首批学位授权自主审核单位、首批工程硕博士培养改革专项试点高校、首批</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“国优计划”试点高校，形成科学研究、产教融合、创新创业的全链条人才培养体系。储能技术、新材料、生物医疗器械、集成电路四个领域获批国家急需高层次人才培养专项。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">成立</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“天津大学学科交叉中心”，先行建设“储能技术”“智能建设与安全”“医工融合”“智能</font><font face="Times New Roman">+</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">”“文化遗产保护”“应用数学”等</font><font face="Times New Roman">6</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">个分中心。</font><font face="Times New Roman">2024</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font><font face="Times New Roman">11</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">月，天津大学正式获批成为教育部学科交叉中心试点建设高校。持续深化医工交叉融合，现有</font><font face="Times New Roman">13</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">家附属医院及医学中心。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">学校科研实力雄厚，聚焦</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“四个面向”，不断强化有组织科研，取得了丰硕成果。获批建设国家重大科技基础设施—大型地震工程模拟研究设施、合成生物学前沿科学中心、天津市国家应用数学中心、天津环渤海滨海地球关键带国家野外科学观测研究站等国家级科研平台。同时，学校坚持面向全球开放办学，全方位深化国际交流合作。与</font><font face="Times New Roman">50</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">个国家和地区的</font><font face="Times New Roman">260</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">所高校、研究所和公司建立了合作关系。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.1000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">一、招生计划</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">天津大学</font>20</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">26</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">普通全日制</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">硕士研究生招生计划约为</font>5</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">2</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">65</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">名</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">（</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New <p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">二、考试方式、报考条件</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">我校</font><font face="Times New Roman">2026</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年初试考试方式：全国统一考试、应届本科毕业生推荐免试、单独考试</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">、</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">免初试攻读硕士研究生</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.1000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">（一）全国统一考试报考条件</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">（考试方式码：</font><font face="Times New Roman">21</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">1</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">中华人民共和国公民。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">拥护中国共产党的领导，遵纪守法</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">，</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">品德良好。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">3</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">身体健康状况符合</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">国家</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">和我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">规定的体检要求。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">4</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">考生的</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">学业水平须</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">符合下列条件之一：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-size: 12pt;">①</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">国家承认学历的应届本科毕业生</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">（含普通高校、成人高校、普通高校举办的成人高等学历教育等应届本科毕业生）及自学考试和网络教育届时可毕业本科生，录取当年（</font><font face="Times New Roman">2026</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font><font face="Times New Roman">9</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">月</font><font face="Times New Roman">1</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">日前</font></span><b><u><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">（下述均以此为准）</span></u></b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">）须取得国家承认的本科毕业证书。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoPlainText" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-size: 12pt;">②</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">具有国家承认的本科毕业学历的人员</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><a Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年继续在西部省、自治区、直辖市和新疆建设兵团招收</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">“</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">少数民族高层次骨干人才培养计划</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">”</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">硕士研究生。报考该计划的考生，必须在网上报名前征得所在省、自治区或直辖市教育厅（教委）民教处（或高教处）同意</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">，</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">并获得</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">审核通过的</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">《</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">少数民族高层次骨干人才</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">计划考生</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">登记表》</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">“</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">少数民族骨干计划</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">”</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">以报名时身份为准，不得更改。详细情况与当地教育主管部门联系。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.1000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">（七）报考</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“退役大学生士兵计划</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">”</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">现役军人报考硕士生，按</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">军队相关部门有关</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">规定办理。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">我校招收</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“退役大学生士兵</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">计划</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">”专项硕士研究生（全日制）。报考“退役大学生士兵</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">计划</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">”专项硕士研究生招生计划的考生，应为高校学生应征入伍退出现役，且符合</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">全国</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">硕士研究生</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">考试</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">报考条件者</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">【</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">高校学生指全日制普通本专科（含高职）、研究生、第二学士学位的应（往）届毕业生、在校生和入学新生，以及成人高校招收的普通本专科（含高职）应（往）届毕业生、在校生和入学新生</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">，</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">下同</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">】</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">。考生报名时应当选择填报退役大学生士兵专项计划，并按要求填报本人入伍前的入学信息以及入伍、退役等相关信息，复试前须向招生单位提供《入伍批准书》和《退出现役证》</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">（限士兵）</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">进行复核。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">审核或复核未通过的，不得按照</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“退役大学生士兵计划”参加复试，仅可在调剂阶段按规定申请调剂到普通计划。报考普通计划的考生，若报名时申请退役大学生士兵初试加分并审核通过，调剂阶段可按规定申请调剂“退役大学生士兵计划”；若报名时未申请退役大学生士兵初试加分的，不可参加“退役大学生士兵计划”调剂。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">（八）</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">对服役期间获得三等战功、二等功以上奖励或者二级以上表彰，符合全国硕士研究生招生考试报考条件的退役人员，可申请免初试攻读硕士研究生。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">符合免初试资格的考生，应在教育部规定的全国统考报名时间内登录</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“全国推荐免试攻读研究生（免初试、转段）信息公开暨管理服务系统”（网址：</font><font face="Times New Roman">https://yz.chsi.com.cn/tm</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）报名，逾期不得补报。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">对审核通过的考生组织复试，复试合格者即可予以拟录取。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent: 24pt; line-height: 17pt;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;mso-list:l0 level1 lfo1;"><!--[if !supportLists]--><span style="font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">三、</span><!--[endif]--><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">报名方式及时间</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">20</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">26</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年硕士研究生招生报名</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">包括</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">网上报名和</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">网上确认</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">报名信息</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">两个阶段。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;&#10;mso-list:l3 level1 lfo2;"><!--[if !supportLists]--><span style="font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">（一）</span><!--[endif]--><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">网上报名</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">1</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">登陆</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">“</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">中国研究生招生信息网</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">”（</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">网址：</font></span><a href="http://yz.chsi.com.cn/"><u><span class="15" style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;">http://yz.chsi.com.cn/</span></u></a><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">或</font></span><a href="http://yz.chsi.cn/"><u><span class="15" style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;">http://yz.chsi.cn/</span></u></a><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）报名。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">网上预报名时间：</font><font face="Times New Roman">20</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">5</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">10</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">月</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">10</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">日至</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">10</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">月</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">1</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">3</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">日，每天</font><font face="Times New Roman">9:00-22:00</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">（预报名生成</font><font face="Times New Roman">9</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">位报名号并</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">缴费成功</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">后，表示网报成功，等同正式报名）。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">正式报名时间：</font><font face="Times New Roman">20</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">5</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font>10<font face="仿宋_GB2312">月</font><font face="Times New Roman">1</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">6</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">日至</font>2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">7</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">日</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，每天</font><font face="Times New Roman">9:00-22:00</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">报考点选择要求：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">①</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-hansi-font-family:仿宋;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">在津参加报名考试的考生，报考点选择的具体要求以天津市</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-hansi-font-family:仿宋;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">教育招生考试院</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-hansi-font-family:仿宋;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">公布的网报公告为准。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">②</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">报考</span><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">单独考试</span></b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">的考生，报名时报考点必须选择</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“天津大学”。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">③</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-hansi-font-family:仿宋;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">其他考生</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">应选择工作</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">所在地</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">或</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">户籍</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">所在地省级教育招生考试机构指定的报考点办理网上报名和网上确认手续。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-size: 12pt;">④</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">报考我校建筑学院</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">（含</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">天津大学</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">福州</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">国际联合</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">学院等自命题使用单位</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">）</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，参加</font><font face="Times New Roman">911</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、</font><font face="Times New Roman">912</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、</font><font face="Times New Roman">913</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">科目考试的考生，报考点请选择天津大学。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;&#10;mso-list:l2 level1 lfo3;"><!--[if !supportLists]--><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">3.&nbsp;</span><!--[endif]--><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">报名期间将对考生学历（学籍）信息进行网上校验，考生可随时上网查看学历（学籍）校验结果。考生也可在报名前或报名期间自行登录</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“中国高等教育学生信息网”（网址：</font></span><a href="http://www.chsi.com.cn/"><u><span class="15" style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">http://www.chsi.com.cn/</font></span></u></a><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">）查询本人学历（学籍）信息。未通过学历（学籍）校验的考生应及时到学籍学历权威认证机构进行认证，在网上确认时将认证报告交报考点核验。</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;&#10;mso-list:l2 level1 lfo3;"><!--[if !supportLists]--><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">4.&nbsp;</span><!--[endif]--><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">符合享受初试加分、少数民族照顾政策、免初试或报考专项计划有关条件的考生，须在网上报名时按要求填报申请信息。有关部门、报考点对相关考生资格进行初审，</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">在复试前进行复审，确定考生的相应资格。其中，申请享受少数民族照顾政策的资格由报考点进行初审，其余资格由有关部门按职责分工进行初审。未按规定申报或审核（含复审）未通过的，不享受相应的加分、照顾、免初试政策，或不得参加有关专项计划初、复试。</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">报考专项计划的考生，不再享受初试加分和少数民族照顾政策。</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;&#10;mso-list:l2 level1 lfo3;"><!--[if !supportLists]--><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">5.&nbsp;</span><!--[endif]--><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">报名期间，考生可自行修改网报信息或重新填报报名信息，但每位考生只能保留一条有效报名信息。逾期不得修改报名信息。</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">考生应当按要求准确填写个人网上报名信息并提供真实材料，严格按照报考条件及相关政策要求填报志愿并选择报考点。考生因网报信息填写错误、填报虚假信息而造成不能网上确认、考试（含初试和复试）或录取的，后果由考生本人承担。</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;&#10;mso-list:l3 level1 lfo2;"><!--[if !supportLists]--><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">（二）</span><!--[endif]--><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">网上确认</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">所有考生（不含推免生）均应在规定时间内根据</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">报考点</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">要求</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">通</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">进行</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">网上确认。</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">具体时间请关注各报考点安排，</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">逾期不再补办。</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">考生网上确认时应当积极配合报考点，根据核验工作，按要求提交有关补充材料。考生应当按规定缴纳报考费，按报考点规定配合采集本人图像等相关电子信息</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">，</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">签署《考生诚信考试承诺书》并遵守相关约定，认真核对并确认本人报名信息，根据核验工作要求提交补充材料，逾期不得补办。在读研究生报考须在报名前征得所在培养单位同意。</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">经考生确认的报名信息在考试（含初试和复试）或录取阶段一律不作修改，</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">考生因网报信息填写错误、填报虚假信息而造成不能考试（含初试、复试）或录取的，后果由考生本人承担。</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><br />
</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．</font> &nbsp;<font face="仿宋_GB2312">（三）准考证打印</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">考前经我校确认准考资格的考生可凭网上报名使用的用户名和密码登录中国研究生招生信息网下载打印《准考证》。《准考证》使用</font><font face="Times New Roman">A4</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">幅面白纸打印，正反两面在使用期间不得涂改或书写。考生凭下载打印的《准考证》及有效居民身份证参加初试和复试</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">。</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">（</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">四</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">）</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;">初试</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">1.</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;">&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">初试时间：</font><font face="Times New Roman">202</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">5</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font><font face="Times New Roman">12</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">月</font><font face="Times New Roman">2</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">0</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">日～</font><font face="Times New Roman">12</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">月</font><font face="Times New Roman">2</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">1</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">日（每天上午</font><font face="Times New Roman">8:30-11:30</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，下午</font><font face="Times New Roman">14:00-17:00</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">。时间以北京时间为准。</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">2</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">. </font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">初试地点：由各报考点另行通知</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; letter-spacing: 0pt; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">.</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">四</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">学习方式</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">1</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．全日制研究生是指符合国家研究生招生规定，通过研究生入学考试或者国家承认的其他入学方式，被具有实施研究生教育资格的高等学校或其他高等教育机构录取，在基本修业年限或者学校规定年限内，全脱产在校学习的研究生。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．非全日制研究生指符合国家研究生招生规定，通过研究生入学考试或者国家承认的其他入学方式，被具有实施研究生教育资格的高等学校或其他高等教育机构录取，在基本修业年限或者学校规定的修业年限（一般应适当延长基本修业年限）内，在从事其他职业或者社会实践的同时，采取多种方式和灵活时间安排进行非脱产学习的研究生。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">全日制和非全日制研究生毕业时，我校根据其修业年限、学业成绩等，按照国家有关规定发给相应的、注明学习方式（全日制、非全日制）的毕业证书；其学业水平达到国家规定的学位标准，可以申请授予相应的学位证书。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">五</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、报考类别</font></span></b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;">1</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">．定</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">向就业</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">定向就业硕士研究生须在被录取前与招生单位、用人单位分别签订定向就业合同，毕业后回定向单位就业。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoPlainText" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．非定向就业</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">所在招生单位推荐，本人与用人单位</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“双向选择”的办法就业。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoPlainText" style="text-indent:24.1000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:宋体;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Courier New';mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">六</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、复试、调剂及录取</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">1</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">复试：凡是</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">进入我校复试名单</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">的考生均应参加复试</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">及复试前资格审查</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，资格审</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">查</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">具体安排见网上通知。复试分数线由我校自行划定，复试时间一般在</font>20</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">26</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年</font>3<font face="仿宋_GB2312">月</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">-4</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">月</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">，复试办法将</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">于</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">复试前在网上公布，复试科目见</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">2026</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年我校硕士研究生</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">招生专业目录，复试笔试科目的考试大纲可查询我校网站</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">阶段</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">主干课程</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">，</span><span
