Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">跨学科专业复试的考生（</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">除同等学力报考以外</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">不</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">需要在复试中加试与报考专业相关的本科主干课程。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">对考生的身份审核及报考专项计划、申请享受初试加分或少数民族照顾政策等资格的复审</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">，</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">对不符合规定条件者，不予复试。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" align="justify" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;text-align:justify;text-justify:inter-ideograph;&#10;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">考生</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">应</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">自觉遵守</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">考场规则及考生所签署的《诚信复试承诺书》等内容，在</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">复试工作结束前不得对外透露或传播复试试题内容等有关情况。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">考生须</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">参加我校统一组织的体检</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">根据《中华人民共和国残疾人教育条例》和《教育部办公厅</font> <font face="仿宋_GB2312">卫生部办公厅关于普通高等学校招生学生入学身体检查取消乙肝项目检测有关问题的通知》（教学厅〔</font><font face="Times New Roman">2010</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">〕</font><font face="Times New Roman">2</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">号）等文件规定，参照《教育部 卫生部 中国残疾人联合会关于印发〈普通高等学校招生体检工作指导意见〉的通知》（教学〔</font><font face="Times New Roman">2003</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">〕</font><font face="Times New Roman">3</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">号）要求</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">开展体检工作</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">。体检时间安排在考生</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">复试结束后</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">组织进行，具体时间另行通知。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">调剂：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">报考我校且</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">符合我校复试分数线而因报考专业已招满时，可根据教育部调剂规定</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">申请</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">调剂到</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">校内</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">生源不足的相近专业。符合教育部复试分数线要求而未被我校录取的考生可根据教育部调剂规定</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">申请</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">调剂到外校。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">3</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">录取：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校将</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">在本单位招委会的统一领导下，按照教育部有关招生政策规定及</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">天津市教育招生考试院</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">的管理和实施办法，根据</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">招生计划、复试录取办法以及考生考试综合成绩、思想政治素质和品德考核情况、身心健康状况等</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">，</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">按照</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">“按需招生、全面衡量、择优录取和宁缺毋滥</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">”</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">的原则</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">择优确定拟录取名单</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">应届本科毕业生及自学考试和网络教育届时可毕业本科生考生，在录取当年入学前须取得国家承认的国（境）内高校本科毕业证书或教育部留学服务中心出具的《国（境）外学历学位认证书》。录取当年入学前未取得相关证书的，其录取资格无效。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">七</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">学费及</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">奖</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">助</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">学金</font></span></b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:Calibri;mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" align="justify" style="text-indent:21.0000pt;mso-pagination:widow-orphan;text-align:justify;&#10;text-justify:inter-ideograph;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">按照国家和天津市的规定收取学费</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">，硕士研究生学费按学制缴纳</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" align="justify" style="text-indent:21.0000pt;mso-pagination:widow-orphan;text-align:justify;&#10;text-justify:inter-ideograph;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">1</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．全日制硕士研究生</font></span></b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" align="justify" style="text-indent:21.0000pt;mso-pagination:widow-orphan;text-align:justify;&#10;text-justify:inter-ideograph;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">2026</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">级全日制硕士研究生收费标准如下：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:Calibri;mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">我校统筹政府投入、学校自筹经费、科研经费、社会捐助等资金，建立健全</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">奖、助、勤、贷、补的奖励资助</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:Calibri;mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">体系。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:Calibri;mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">奖励体系包括国家奖学金、学业奖学金、专项奖学金、单项优秀学生奖等。国家奖学金用于奖励学业成绩特别优秀、科学研究成果显著、社会公益活动表现突出的全日制硕士研究生，标准为每生每年</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">20000</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">元。学业奖学金特等奖最高可达每生每年</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">12000</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">元（具体以当年发布的评审方案为准）。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">资助体系包括国家助学贷款、研究生助学金、助管基金、助教基金、国际交流基金、专项助学金等。其中全日制</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">非定向就业</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">硕士研究生</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">及少数民族骨干研究生</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">助学金为每生</font><font face="Times New Roman">6000</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">元</font><font face="Times New Roman">/</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年以及导师助研两部分组成，助学金覆盖面可达</font><font face="Times New Roman">100%</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:Calibri;mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">适用对象为纳入全国研究生招生计划的取得正式学籍的全日制</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">硕士</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:Calibri;mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">研究生（有固定工资收入的除外）。具体执行细则参见当年的校发文件。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:Calibri;mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">（如有变动，请以天津大学最新文件为准）</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" align="justify" style="text-indent:21.0000pt;mso-pagination:widow-orphan;text-align:justify;&#10;text-justify:inter-ideograph;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．非全日制硕士研究生</span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">2026</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">级非全日制硕士研究生收费标准如下：</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:Calibri;&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:Calibri;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">除上表所列学院及专业外，其他学院的所有专业学制均为</font>3<font face="仿宋_GB2312">年。新生入学后双向选择指导教师。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．研究生因特殊原因未能在规定的学习年限内完成学业，或为了更深入开展科学研究，可申请适当延长学习年限但不能超过学校规定的最长学习年限。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>

            <td width="220" valign="center" style="width: 82.5pt; padding: 0pt 5.4pt; border-left: none; border-right: 1pt solid windowtext; border-top: none; border-bottom: 1pt solid windowtext; background-image: initial; background-position: initial; background-size: initial; background-repeat: initial; background-attachment: initial; background-origin: initial; background-clip: initial;">
            <p class="MsoNormal" align="center" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;text-align:center;&#10;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">1357</span><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
            <p class="MsoNormal" align="center" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;text-align:center;&#10;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">设计</font></span><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
            </td>
            <td width="214" valign="bottom" nowrap="" style="width: 80.25pt; padding: 0pt 5.4pt; border-left: none; border-right: 1pt solid windowtext; border-top: none; border-bottom: 1pt solid windowtext; background-image: initial; background-position: initial; background-size: initial; background-repeat: initial; background-attachment: initial; background-origin: initial; background-clip: initial;">
            <p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">　</font></span><span style="font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>

            <td width="188" valign="bottom" nowrap="" style="width: 70.85pt; padding: 0pt 5.4pt; border-left: none; border-right: 1pt solid windowtext; border-top: none; border-bottom: 1pt solid windowtext; background-image: initial; background-position: initial; background-size: initial; background-repeat: initial; background-attachment: initial; background-origin: initial; background-clip: initial;">
            <p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">　</font></span><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>

            <p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">　</font></span><span style="font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">3</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">根据国家有关规定，录取</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">为全日制的学</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">生户口迁移实行自愿原则。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">4</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．符合报考地处二区招生单位且毕业后在国务院公布的民族区域自治地方定向就业的少数民族普通高校应届本科毕业生；或者</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">报名时</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">工作单位和户籍在国务院公布的民族区域自治地方，且定向就业单位为原单位的少数民族在职人员</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">考生，可按规定</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">申请享受</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">少数民族</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">照顾政策，须在网上报名时按要求填报相关信息，并如实填写少数民族身份及定向就业少数民族地区。报考点对相关考生资格进行初审，招生单位在复试（含调剂）前进行复审。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">申请享受少数民族照顾政策的考生，不再享受初试加分政策。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;&#10;mso-list:l1 level1 lfo4;"><!--[if !supportLists]--><span style="font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">5.&nbsp;</span><!--[endif]--><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">符合下列条件之一后</font><font face="Times New Roman">3</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年内报名参加全国硕士研究生招生考试（不含单独考试）的考生，可申请享受初试总成绩加</font><font face="Times New Roman">10</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">分，同等条件下优先录取。符合多项加分项目的考生，分值不得累加。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">①</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">参加</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“大学生志愿服务西部计划”“三支一扶计划”“农村义务教育阶段学校教师特设岗位计划”“国际中文教育志愿者”项目之一，服务期满且考核合格。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-size: 12pt;">②</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">退役大学生士兵达到全国硕士研究生招生考试报考条件。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">6</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．在复试、录取阶段，将在我校研招网向社会公布本校复试录取办法和各院系实施细则，各院（系、所）或学科、专业招生人数，参加复试考生名单（包括考生姓名、考生编号、初试各科成绩等信息）和拟录取考生名单（包括考生姓名、考生编号、初试成绩、复试成绩、总成绩等信息），公示时间</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">不少于</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">7</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">日。对参加专项计划、享受初试加分或照顾政策的考生相关情况，在公布考生名单时进行说明，我校无破格复试和录取。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">7</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">．</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">我校</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">2</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">02</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">6</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年招生专业目录及</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">自命题考试科目大纲可在我校研究生招生网</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">站（网址：</span><a href="http://yzb.tju.edu.cn/"><u><span class="15" style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 12pt;">http://yzb.tju.edu.cn</span></u></a><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">）</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">上查询</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">。我校不举办考前辅导班、不提供历年真题试卷。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">8</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．我校建筑工程学院、管理与经济学部和人工智能学院部分团队</font><font face="Times New Roman">2026</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年拟申请海南招生专项指标，该专项录取考生培养过程需在海南完成，校内不提供住宿。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">9</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">.</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">已被</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">录取的推免生，不得再报名参加当年全国统一考试、单独考试。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">10. </font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ascii-font-family:'Times New Roman';&#10;mso-hansi-font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-bidi-font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">对于拟录取为定向就业的考生，一经录取后，不得变更录取信息中的就业方式。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">11</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">.</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">考生不得伪造有关证明。一经发现作伪，将取消其考试资格、录取资格或取消其学籍</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">十、部分学院</font><font face="Times New Roman">/</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">专业报考要求</font></span></b><b><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-ansi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">1</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．报考机械工程学院</font><font face="Times New Roman">085500</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">机械（专业学位）</font><a name="_Hlk113547839"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">的考生请注意：</font></a><font face="Times New Roman">04</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">智能装备与医疗机器人和</font><font face="Times New Roman">05</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">创新设计与智能制造两个研究方向基本学习年限均为三年，第一年统一在天津大学校本部（卫津路</font><font face="Times New Roman">/</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">北洋园校区）完成部分课程学习，后两年分别入驻各自的研究院开展课题研究、撰写学位论文等。具体说明及导师信息请见</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">天津大学</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">机械工程学院官网</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">—</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">学院公告</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">（网址：</font><font face="Times New Roman">http://me.tju.edu.cn/index.jsp</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">报考</font><font face="Times New Roman">080100</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">力学专业的</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">考生请注意：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">在复试阶段，将按照力学高超声速边界层流动稳定性和转捩方向、力学其他方向征集志愿，并按照上述两个方向组织复试录取工作。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;&#10;mso-list:l4 level1 lfo5;"><!--[if !supportLists]--><span style="font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">2．</span><!--[endif]--><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">报考建筑工程学院</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">“土木工程”专业（含岩土工程、结构工程、防灾减灾工程及防护工程、桥梁与隧道工程、工程技术与管理）的考生请选择“</font><font face="Times New Roman">081400</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">土木工程”。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>

<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">参加</font><font face="Times New Roman">911</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、</font><font face="Times New Roman">912</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、</font><font face="Times New Roman">913</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">科目考试的考生，报考点请选择天津大学。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">详细情况以准考证及研招办网上通知为准。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">4</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．管理与经济学部</font><font face="Times New Roman">125100</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">工商管理</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">（专业学位）专业</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">包括工商管理（</font><font face="Times New Roman">MBA</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）方向和高级工商管理（</font><font face="Times New Roman">EMBA</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">）方向，其中</font><font face="Times New Roman">01</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">为工商管理全日制（全英文教学），</font><font face="Times New Roman">02</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">为工商管理非全日制，</font><font face="Times New Roman">03</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">为高级工商管理非全日制，具体参考管理与经济学部工商管理硕士和高级工商管理硕士招生简章。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">5</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．报考理学院</font><font face="Times New Roman">085600</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">材料与化工（专业学位）的考生请注意：该专业为天津大学</font><font face="Times New Roman">-</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">中国科学院杭州医学研究所联合培养专项。该专业的基本学习年限为三年，第一年统一在天津大学校本部（卫津路</font><font face="Times New Roman">/</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">北洋园校区）完成部分课程学习，后两年入驻中国科学院杭州医学研究所开展课题研究、撰写学位论文等。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;mso-bidi-font-weight:bold;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">6</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．药物科学与技术学院</font><font face="Times New Roman">100700</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">药学</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">专业</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">、</font><font face="Times New Roman">105500</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">药学（专业学位）</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">专业</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">采用全英文教学。</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">7</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．报考医学院</font><font face="Times New Roman">100200</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">临床医学专业、</font><font face="Times New Roman">105100</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">临床医学（专业学位）专业的考生请注意：</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">①</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">请查询医学院官网通知公告栏，关注临床医学专业介绍与报名条件；</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-size: 12pt;">②</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">在复试阶段将进行二级方向</font><font face="Times New Roman">/</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">专业领域志愿征集，并按照方向组织复试录取工作。</font><font face="Times New Roman">100200</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">临床医学专业可选二级学科包括内科学、儿科学、神经病学、精神病与精神卫生学、影像医学与核医学、临床检验诊断学、外科学、眼科学、肿瘤学、康复医学与理疗学、麻醉学。</font><font face="Times New Roman">105100</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">临床医学（专业学位）专业可选专业领域包括内科学、儿科学、神经病学、精神病与精神卫生学、重症医学、外科学、儿外科学、妇产科学、耳鼻咽喉科学、麻醉学、放射影像学、急诊医学。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="font-size: 12pt;">③</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">105100</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">临床医学（专业学位）专业，学生住宿由所选的规培基地安排，校内不提供住宿。若基地因条件所限无法安排宿舍，则需要学生自行解决住宿。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">8</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">．</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">我校非全日制招生专业（</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">工商管理、公共管理、会计硕士、工程管理和单独考试专业</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">除外）均安排在</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="Times New Roman">2</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">36</span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:仿宋_GB2312;font-size:12.0000pt;&#10;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><font face="仿宋_GB2312">专业学位教育中心招生、培养，其培养方案（含学费、学制）参照所报考专业研究方向对应学院执行。具体专业以《天津大学</font><font face="Times New Roman">2026</font><font face="仿宋_GB2312">年硕士学位研究生招生专业目录》为准。</font></span><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="text-indent:24.0000pt;mso-char-indent-count:2.0000;mso-layout-grid-align:none;&#10;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="p" align="justify" style="margin-top:3.7500pt;margin-bottom:3.7500pt;text-align:justify;&#10;text-justify:inter-ideograph;"><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><font face="Arial">&nbsp;</font></span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;">本简章由我校负责解释，未尽事宜或与教育部规定不一致的，以教育部文件为准。</span><span style="font-family: 仿宋_GB2312; font-size: 12pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" style="mso-layout-grid-align:none;layout-grid-mode:char;line-height:17.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><span style="mso-spacerun:'yes';font-family:'Times New Roman';mso-fareast-font-family:仿宋_GB2312;&#10;font-size:12.0000pt;mso-font-kerning:1.0000pt;">&nbsp;</span></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" align="justify" style="text-align:justify;text-justify:inter-ideograph;line-height:20.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 15pt;">&nbsp;</span></b></p>
<p class="MsoNormal" align="justify" style="text-align:justify;text-justify:inter-ideograph;line-height:20.0000pt;&#10;mso-line-height-rule:exactly;"><b><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 15pt;"><font face="微软雅黑">天津大学研究生招生办公室</font></span></b><b><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 15pt;"><o:p></o:p></span></b></p>
